2026-10-17T08:50:36.6283929Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Measure the warp-redux-r176 Decode quantizer on the P-round-robin, cap-31 runtime

One-factor kernel experiment on top of d93ebab (prefill round_robin
dispatch, decode cap 31 per DP rank, extra slots 17, concurrency 192,
Actions 39018316844 is its reference run). The only change is a sixth
DeepGEMM patch, patches/deep-gemm/sm90-decode-warp-redux.patch, a
byte-identical copy of komoriyuta's research/kernel-ablation/
2026-10-17-decode/warp-redux-r176.patch (SHA-256 d4fba8a9...).

What the patch changes in the SM90 MegaMoE kernel: in the SwapAB FC1
epilogue quantizer (Qwen H4096/I1536/EP4 decode and the small-prefill
SwapAB fallback), the producer stores SwiGLU FP32 values in an
XOR-swizzled shared layout and one warp per token row reads two
columns per lane and reduces the group-64 amax with one full-warp
REDUX, instead of one thread per row doing 64 serial loads and 32
serial FP8 pair packs with at most 64 of 256 threads active. The math
register budget rises from 168 to 176 (CTA 50688 of 64512) to remove
the 8-byte spills the 168-register variant showed. CTA barrier count
(29), FP8 E4M3 format, FP32 scale formula, SiLU, clamping, top-k and
K accumulation order are unchanged; the rejected weight-scale cache of
21c2927 is not included. komoriyuta's CPU evidence: zero decode spills,
+4.4% static instructions, CPU layout model passes for all 0..64 live
rows (check_quant_layout.py PASS on this tree).

Wiring follows 21c2927: Dockerfile COPY, build-sm90-megamoe.py applies
the patch sixth and records decode_warp_redux_patch_sha256,
build-sglang-container.sh copies it into the context,
check-firmus-image.sh pins the new header SHA-256 710bdf40... and the
patch SHA, and test_firmus_image.py pins both with a corruption case.
The header chain was reproduced on CPU from the pinned sgl-deep-gemm
0.1.5 wheel (e5e9f92c...): a53d83ca... -> five chain patches ->
0e34a083... -> this patch -> 710bdf40..., all hunks applying without
rejects (GNU patch; the image build is the first git apply).

Why this candidate first: it is the smallest delta from the measured
kernel among komoriyuta's two preferred candidates, keeps the data path
and barrier structure, and does not share the 192-register budget or
register-resident values of the rejected combined quantizer, so a
regression would not be ambiguous. The decode MegaMoE kernel is 63% of
the 38 ms decode step and the ITL slope shows the step is dominated by
per-row work, which is what this quantizer touches. The expected gain is
unproven; this run attributes it.

Acceptance: image sm90-megamoe-build.json header 710bdf40..., provision
check-firmus-image.sh PASS, the finite GPU numeric gate PASS before
serving (all SwapAB widths, full tile and tail, zero input), official
validation valid and eligible, then per-rank decode MegaMoE time per
step and ITL at matched running against the d93ebab run; score against
6521.98 is reported but not treated as conclusive from one run. A
regression is reverted as 2a6a096 did. komoriyuta's ablation record
predates today's authorization of GPU runs for this series and is left
as written. Configs, sglang.sbatch and the GPU gate are unchanged;
provisioning replaces the image pin for this [require-build] run.
Ruff, shell syntax and the full test suite pass. Implementation by a
delegated agent, reviewed before submission.
